- **aio-message-bridge**: Give Claude Code an event loop. A local HTTP + WebSocket relay plus the Monitor tool let anything outside Claude — a CLI, a mobile app, a webhook, another program, a web page — talk to it mid-task in both directions. Inbound: client POSTs an event, relay prints a MSG:: line to stdout, Monitor surfaces it as a notification. Outbound: Claude POSTs a push, relay broadcasts it over WebSocket. Generic, language-agnostic protocol with a ready-to-run reference relay, plus optional token-gated network exposure over Cloudflare Tunnel, Tailscale, or LAN. ([aiocean/claude-plugins](https://github.com/aiocean/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/aio-message-bridge) · ★4 · 2 skills)
